<script id="ethvelle-text-workflow-v10-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let samplerActive=false, activePointer=null, savedVis=[], samplePickerWasOpen=false;
  const hexColor=v=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():null;

  // Add a headline quick-start and ordinary custom-text entry to the existing Media sheet.
  function addTextTiles(){
    try{
      if(typeof panelContent==='undefined'||!panelContent.media||typeof panelContent.media.body!=='string')return;
      const body=panelContent.media.body;
      if(body.includes('ev-text-add-tile'))return;
      const pos=body.lastIndexOf('</div>');
      if(pos<0)return;
      const tiles=`
        <div class="option-tile ev-text-add-tile" onclick="closePanel();openEthvelleTextComposer()">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7V4h16v3M9 20h6M12 4v16"/></svg><span>Text</span>
        </div>
`;
      panelContent.media.body=body.slice(0,pos)+tiles+body.slice(pos);
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  const priorOpenComposer=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  // Keep the text-entry view uncluttered like Picsart: keyboard + Font/Bold/Stroke/Shadow/BG
  // + swatches. The extended selected-layer dock stays reserved for the post-entry edit mode.
  if(typeof priorOpenComposer==='function'){
    window.openEthvelleTextComposer=function(){
      /* No-flash open: the styling dock (Font tab etc.) is moved into the composer and briefly
         set to display:flex by the layers below. Keep it hidden through CSS for the whole
         synchronous open so the browser never paints it, then release the guard. */
      document.body.classList.add('evtc-opening');
      let result;
      try{result=priorOpenComposer.apply(this,arguments);}
      catch(err){document.body.classList.remove('evtc-opening');throw err;}
      {const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
        if(root&&root.classList.contains('active')&&dock)dock.style.display='none';}
      window.setTimeout(()=>{
        const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
        if(root&&root.classList.contains('active')){
          root.querySelectorAll('.evtc-dock-tools,#evtcColorStrip,.evtc-opacity-line').forEach(el=>{el.style.display='';});
          if(dock)dock.style.display='none';
        }
        document.body.classList.remove('evtc-opening');
      },0);
      return result;
    };
  }
  window.ethvelleOpenHeadlineComposer=function(){
    try{if(typeof closePanel==='function')closePanel();}catch(_e){}
    if(typeof window.openEthvelleTextComposer==='function')window.openEthvelleTextComposer();
    window.setTimeout(()=>{
      const input=$('evtcStageText');
      try{
        if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function'){
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontFamily','Sora');
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontSize',64);
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('fontWeight',800);
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textAlign','center');
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textColor','#FFFFFF');
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textBackgroundEnabled',false);
          window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textCurve',0);
        }
      }catch(_e){}
      if(input){input.placeholder='Write your headline';input.setAttribute('aria-label','Write your headline');input.focus({preventScroll:true});}
    },140);
  };

  // Make the existing shared styling dock consistently behave and look like a bottom sheet.
  function decorateDock(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    if(!dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-head')){
      const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='evpa-sheet-head';
      head.innerHTML='<span class="evpa-sheet-grabber" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="evpa-sheet-title">Text</span><button type="button" class="evpa-sheet-close" aria-label="Close text settings">×</button>';
      dock.insertBefore(head,dock.firstChild);
      head.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click',()=>{
        dock.style.display='none';
        const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(row){row.style.visibility='';row.style.pointerEvents='';}
        document.querySelectorAll('.ev-text-tool').forEach(el=>el.style.display='flex');
      });
    }
    const active=dock.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active');
    const name=active?(active.querySelector('span')?.textContent||active.getAttribute('aria-label')||active.dataset.tab||'Text'):'Text';
    const title=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-title');if(title&&title.textContent!==name)title.textContent=name;
  }
  function installDockObserver(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    decorateDock();
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>decorateDock());
    observer.observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});
  }

  function stageContains(el,stage){return !!el&&!!stage&&(el===stage||stage.contains(el));}
  function imagePixel(el,x,y){
    try{
      if(!el||!(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'))return null;
      const nw=el.tagName==='VIDEO'?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=el.tagName==='VIDEO'?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);
      if(!nw||!nh||el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.readyState<2)return null;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el),fit=cs.objectFit||'fill';let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;
      if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'){
        const scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);
        const rw=nw*scale,rh=nh*scale,ox=(r.width-rw)/2,oy=(r.height-rh)/2;
        sx=(x-r.left-ox)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-oy)/scale;
      }
      sx=Math.max(0,Math.min(nw-1,Math.floor(sx)));sy=Math.max(0,Math.min(nh-1,Math.floor(sy)));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});ctx.drawImage(el,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);
      const d=ctx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;if(d[3]<8)return null;
      return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function canvasPixel(el,x,y){
    try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||!el.width||!el.height)return null;
      const px=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.width-1,Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width))),py=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.height-1,Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height)));
      const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(!d||d[3]<8)return null;
      return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function backgroundPixel(el){
    try{
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);const m=String(cs.backgroundColor||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)(?:\s*,\s*([\d.]+))?\s*\)/i);
      if(m&&(m[4]===undefined||Number(m[4])>.08))return '#'+m.slice(1,4).map(v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Number(v))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
      // For text pixels, use its rendered foreground color when the exact glyph box is under the picker.
      if(el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){
        const c=String(cs.color||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)/i);if(c)return '#'+c.slice(1,4).map(v=>Number(v).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    return null;
  }
  function readStageColor(x,y){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return null;
    let stack=[];try{stack=document.elementsFromPoint(x,y).filter(el=>stageContains(el,stage));}catch(_e){}
    // Prefer real rendered media/paint canvases under any overlaid text layer.
    for(const el of stack){if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){const c=canvasPixel(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}}
    for(const el of stack){if(el.tagName==='VIDEO'||el.tagName==='IMG'){const c=imagePixel(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}}
    for(const el of stack){const c=backgroundPixel(el);if(c)return c;}
    let n=stack[0]||stage;while(n&&stage.contains(n)){const c=backgroundPixel(n);if(c)return c;if(n===stage)break;n=n.parentElement;}
    // A plain canvas-stage background is a useful last fallback when its color is solid.
    return backgroundPixel(stage);
  }
  function pickerKey(){
    const active=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'color';
    return active==='stroke'?'strokeColor':active==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(['highlight','background'].includes(active)?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor');
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    if(samplerActive)return;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    samplerActive=true;activePointer=null;
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker'),dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock'),stylePanel=$('ethvelleTextStylePanel'),composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
    samplePickerWasOpen=!!picker?.classList.contains('active');
    const roots=[composer,picker,stylePanel,dock].filter(Boolean);savedVis=roots.map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility}));
    roots.forEach(el=>el.style.visibility='hidden');
    let loupe=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!loupe){loupe=document.createElement('div');loupe.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';loupe.innerHTML='<i aria-hidden="true"></i><b class="evpa-loupe-plus" aria-hidden="true">+</b>';document.body.appendChild(loupe);}else if(!loupe.querySelector('b'))loupe.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<b class="evpa-loupe-plus" aria-hidden="true">+</b>');
    loupe.style.display='block';loupe.style.backgroundColor='#777777';
    const updateLoupe=(x,y)=>{loupe.style.left=x+'px';loupe.style.top=y+'px';const c=readStageColor(x,y);if(c)loupe.style.backgroundColor=c;};
    const cleanup=(restore=true)=>{
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',begin,true);document.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);document.removeEventListener('pointerup',end,true);document.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);
      loupe.style.display='none';activePointer=null;samplerActive=false;
      if(restore)savedVis.forEach(o=>{o.el.style.visibility=o.visibility;});
      const d=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(d&&$('bottomToolsClip')?.style.display==='none')d.style.display='flex';
      if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')try{window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(_e){}
      if(samplePickerWasOpen&&picker){picker.classList.add('active');picker.style.visibility=savedVis.find(o=>o.el===picker)?.visibility||'';}
      savedVis=[];
    };
    const begin=e=>{if(activePointer!==null)return;if(!stage.contains(document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY)))return;activePointer=e.pointerId;try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();}catch(_e){}updateLoupe(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const move=e=>{if(activePointer===null||e.pointerId!==activePointer)return;try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();}catch(_e){}updateLoupe(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const end=e=>{
      if(activePointer===null||e.pointerId!==activePointer)return;
      try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();}catch(_e){}
      const x=e.clientX,y=e.clientY;loupe.style.display='none';const sampled=readStageColor(x,y);const key=pickerKey();
      const reopenPicker=samplePickerWasOpen;
      if(sampled&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){
        window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,sampled);
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});
      }else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This pixel could not be read; try another visible area',{duration:1500});
      cleanup(true);
      // If the eyedropper was launched from the custom picker, reopen that same panel with
      // its hue/brightness indicators and preview synchronized to the newly sampled color.
      if(sampled&&reopenPicker&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')window.setTimeout(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key),40);
    };
    const cancel=()=>cleanup(true);
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown',begin,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointerup',end,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Touch and move over a canvas color; release to apply',{duration:1900});
  };

  // Text composer remains a deliberate action: adding text opens it; selecting an existing text
  // layer only selects it. The composer appears again only through Edit text or a double-tap.
  // The existing text-node click handler already selects once and opens the composer on dblclick.
  function keepComposerIntentional(){
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(!layer||layer.dataset.evTextIntentV10==='1')return;
    layer.dataset.evTextIntentV10='1';
    layer.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const node=e.target.closest?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');if(!node)return;
      // Do not open the keyboard as a side effect of selecting a text object.
      // Existing dblclick and explicit Edit text actions continue to open it.
    },true);
  }
  function initialise(){addTextTiles();installDockObserver();keepComposerIntentional();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initialise,{once:true});else initialise();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-editing-reliability-v11">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const GRAD_KEY='ethvelleCustomTextGradients', TEX_KEY='ethvelleCustomTextTextures';
  const validHex=v=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():'#FFFFFF';
  const cleanName=(v,fallback)=>String(v||fallback||'Custom').replace(/[&<>"']/g,'').trim().slice(0,24)||fallback||'Custom';
  function safeCss(v){const s=String(v||'').trim();return /gradient\(/i.test(s)&&/^[#a-zA-Z0-9(),.%\s+-]+$/.test(s)?s:'';}
  function readList(key){try{const a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');return Array.isArray(a)?a.filter(x=>x&&safeCss(x.css)).slice(0,16):[];}catch(_e){return [];}}
  function writeList(key,items){try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(items.slice(0,16)));return true;}catch(_e){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Unable to save custom preset on this device');return false;}}
  function target(){try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function getType(){return $('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab==='texture'?'texture':'gradient';}

  // Saved custom presets are applied through the same public setter as built-in presets.
  window.ethvellePicsartApplyCustomGradient=function(index){const g=readList(GRAD_KEY)[Number(index)];if(g&&safeCss(g.css))window.ethvellePicsartSetGradient(g.css);};
  window.ethvellePicsartApplyCustomTexture=function(index){const t=readList(TEX_KEY)[Number(index)];if(t&&safeCss(t.css))window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture(t.css,/^(100% 100%|\d{1,3}px \d{1,3}px)$/.test(String(t.size||''))?t.size:'100% 100%');};

  // A native range must finish with a committed value; the outline toggle needs a real width.
  const oldSetValue=window.ethvellePicsartSetValue;
  if(typeof oldSetValue==='function'&&!oldSetValue.__ethvelleV11){
    const wrapped=function(key,value,unit,live){
      if(key==='strokeEnabled'){
        const enabled=!!value;
        oldSetValue.call(this,'strokeEnabled',enabled,unit,live);
        const t=target()||{};const width=Math.max(.5,Number(t.strokeWidth)||3);
        oldSetValue.call(this,'strokeWidth',enabled?width:0,'',live);
        return;
      }
      return oldSetValue.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV11=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetValue=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSetGradient=window.ethvellePicsartSetGradient;
  if(typeof oldSetGradient==='function'&&!oldSetGradient.__ethvelleV11){
    const wrapped=function(css){const r=oldSetGradient.apply(this,arguments);if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textTextureSize','100% 100%');return r;};wrapped.__ethvelleV11=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetGradient=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSetTexture=window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture;
  if(typeof oldSetTexture==='function'&&!oldSetTexture.__ethvelleV11){
    const wrapped=function(css,size){const r=oldSetTexture.call(this,css,size||'100% 100%');if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textTextureSize',size||'100% 100%');return r;};wrapped.__ethvelleV11=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture=wrapped;
  }

  let editor=null,editorKind='gradient',stops=[{color:'#FF512F',pos:0},{color:'#F09819',pos:100}],seq=0;
  let angle=90,pattern='stripes',texColor='#FFFFFF',texColor2='#9EA8FF',thickness=2,spacing=12,name='My Gradient';
  function ensureEditor(){
    if(editor)return editor;
    editor=document.createElement('div');editor.id='evpaCustomEffectEditor';editor.className='evce-root';editor.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.appendChild(editor);
    editor.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===editor||e.target.closest('[data-evce-action="cancel"]'))closeEditor();});
    editor.addEventListener('input',e=>{readEditorInputs();updatePreview();});
    editor.addEventListener('change',e=>{readEditorInputs();updatePreview();});
    editor.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const add=e.target.closest('[data-evce-action="add-stop"]');
      const rem=e.target.closest('[data-evce-action="remove-stop"]');
      const action=e.target.closest('[data-evce-action="apply"],[data-evce-action="save"]');
      if(add){readEditorInputs();if(stops.length<6){const last=stops[stops.length-1];stops.splice(stops.length-1,0,{color:seq++%2?'#5B6CFF':'#22D3EE',pos:Math.round((Number(last.pos)+Number(stops[stops.length-2]?.pos||0))/2)});renderEditor();}return;}
      if(rem){readEditorInputs();const i=Number(rem.dataset.index);if(stops.length>2&&Number.isInteger(i)){stops.splice(i,1);renderEditor();}return;}
      if(action){readEditorInputs();applyEditor(action.dataset.evceAction==='save');}
    });
    return editor;
  }
  function currentGradient(){const sorted=stops.map((s,i)=>({color:validHex(s.color),pos:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(s.pos)||0)),i}));return `linear-gradient(${Math.max(0,Math.min(360,Number(angle)||0))}deg, ${sorted.map(s=>`${s.color} ${s.pos}%`).join(', ')})`;}
  function currentTexture(){
    const a=validHex(texColor),b=validHex(texColor2),ang=Math.max(0,Math.min(360,Number(angle)||0)),th=Math.max(1,Math.min(12,Number(thickness)||2)),sp=Math.max(4,Math.min(80,Number(spacing)||12));
    if(pattern==='dots')return {css:`radial-gradient(circle, ${a} 0 ${th}px, transparent ${th+0.7}px)`,size:`${sp}px ${sp}px`};
    if(pattern==='checker')return {css:`repeating-conic-gradient(${a} 0% 25%, ${b} 0% 50%)`,size:`${sp}px ${sp}px`};
    if(pattern==='crosshatch')return {css:`repeating-linear-gradient(${ang}deg, ${a} 0 ${th}px, transparent ${th}px ${sp}px),repeating-linear-gradient(${ang+90}deg, ${b} 0 ${th}px, transparent ${th}px ${sp}px)`,size:'100% 100%'};
    if(pattern==='mesh')return {css:`repeating-linear-gradient(${ang}deg, ${a} 0 1px, transparent 1px ${sp}px),repeating-linear-gradient(${ang+90}deg, ${a} 0 1px, transparent 1px ${sp}px)`,size:'100% 100%'};
    return {css:`repeating-linear-gradient(${ang}deg, ${a} 0 ${th}px, transparent ${th}px ${sp}px)`,size:'100% 100%'};
  }
  function updatePreview(){
    if(!editor?.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const preview=$('evcePreview');if(!preview)return;
    const css=editorKind==='gradient'?currentGradient():currentTexture().css;
    const size=editorKind==='gradient'?'100% 100%':currentTexture().size;
    preview.style.backgroundImage=safeCss(css)||'none';preview.style.backgroundSize=size;
    const out=$('evceAngleValue');if(out)out.textContent=`${angle}°`;
    editor.querySelectorAll('[data-stop-pos]').forEach(inp=>{const out=inp.parentElement.querySelector('output');if(out)out.textContent=inp.value+'%';});
    const thout=$('evceThicknessValue');if(thout)thout.textContent=thickness+' px';const spout=$('evceSpacingValue');if(spout)spout.textContent=spacing+' px';
  }
  function readEditorInputs(){
    const n=$('evceName');if(n)name=cleanName(n.value,editorKind==='gradient'?'My Gradient':'My Texture');
    const an=$('evceAngle');if(an)angle=Number(an.value)||0;
    if(editorKind==='gradient'){
      stops=Array.from(editor.querySelectorAll('.evce-stop')).map((row,i)=>({color:validHex(row.querySelector('[data-stop-color]')?.value),pos:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(row.querySelector('[data-stop-pos]')?.value==null?(i?100:0):row.querySelector('[data-stop-pos]').value)))}));
      if(stops.length<2)stops=[{color:'#FF512F',pos:0},{color:'#F09819',pos:100}];
    }else{
      pattern=$('evcePattern')?.value||'stripes';texColor=validHex($('evceTexColor')?.value);texColor2=validHex($('evceTexColor2')?.value);thickness=Number($('evceThickness')?.value)||2;spacing=Number($('evceSpacing')?.value)||12;
    }
  }
  function renderEditor(){
    const root=ensureEditor();
    const rows=editorKind==='gradient'?`<div class="evce-label-line"><strong>Color stops</strong><button type="button" class="evce-btn" data-evce-action="add-stop" ${stops.length>=6?'disabled':''}>＋ Add stop</button></div><div class="evce-stops">${stops.map((s,i)=>`<div class="evce-stop"><strong>Stop ${i+1}</strong><input type="color" data-stop-color="${i}" value="${validHex(s.color)}" aria-label="Stop ${i+1} color"><input type="range" data-stop-pos="${i}" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(s.pos)||0))}" aria-label="Stop ${i+1} position"><output>${s.pos}%</output><button type="button" data-evce-action="remove-stop" data-index="${i}" aria-label="Remove stop" ${stops.length<=2?'disabled':''}>−</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="evce-field"><label for="evcePattern">Pattern</label><select id="evcePattern"><option value="stripes" ${pattern==='stripes'?'selected':''}>Stripes</option><option value="dots" ${pattern==='dots'?'selected':''}>Dots</option><option value="checker" ${pattern==='checker'?'selected':''}>Checker</option><option value="crosshatch" ${pattern==='crosshatch'?'selected':''}>Crosshatch</option><option value="mesh" ${pattern==='mesh'?'selected':''}>Mesh grid</option></select></div><div class="evce-two-col"><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceTexColor">Pattern color</label><input id="evceTexColor" type="color" value="${validHex(texColor)}"></div><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceTexColor2">Second color</label><input id="evceTexColor2" type="color" value="${validHex(texColor2)}"></div></div><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceThickness">Line / dot thickness <output id="evceThicknessValue">${thickness} px</output></label><input id="evceThickness" type="range" min="1" max="12" value="${thickness}"></div><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceSpacing">Pattern spacing <output id="evceSpacingValue">${spacing} px</output></label><input id="evceSpacing" type="range" min="4" max="80" value="${spacing}"></div>`;
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evce-sheet"><div class="evce-grabber"></div><div class="evce-head"><div><h3>${editorKind==='gradient'?'Custom text gradient':'Custom text texture'}</h3><p>${editorKind==='gradient'?'Choose the angle, colors and position of each stop.':'Build a repeatable pattern and control its scale.'}</p></div><button type="button" class="evce-close" data-evce-action="cancel" aria-label="Close">×</button></div><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceName">Preset name</label><input id="evceName" type="text" maxlength="24" value="${cleanName(name,editorKind==='gradient'?'My Gradient':'My Texture')}"></div><div class="evce-field"><label for="evceAngle">${editorKind==='gradient'?'Gradient angle':'Pattern angle'} <output id="evceAngleValue">${angle}°</output></label><input id="evceAngle" type="range" min="0" max="360" value="${angle}"></div>${rows}<div id="evcePreview" class="evce-preview"></div><div class="evce-actions"><button type="button" class="evce-btn" data-evce-action="cancel">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="evce-btn" data-evce-action="apply">Apply</button><button type="button" class="evce-btn primary" data-evce-action="save">Save preset + apply</button></div></div>`;
    root.classList.add('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');updatePreview();
  }
  function closeEditor(){if(editor){editor.classList.remove('active');editor.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}}
  window.openEthvelleCustomGradientEditor=function(kind){
    editorKind=kind==='texture'?'texture':'gradient';angle=editorKind==='gradient'?90:45;name=editorKind==='gradient'?'My Gradient':'My Texture';
    stops=[{color:'#FF512F',pos:0},{color:'#F09819',pos:100}];pattern='stripes';texColor='#FFFFFF';texColor2='#9EA8FF';thickness=2;spacing=12;
    const t=target()||{};if(editorKind==='gradient'&&t.textGradient){name='Custom Gradient';}if(editorKind==='texture'&&t.textTexture){name='Custom Texture';}
    renderEditor();
  };
  function applyEditor(savePreset){
    readEditorInputs();let entry;
    if(editorKind==='gradient')entry={name:cleanName(name,'My Gradient'),css:currentGradient(),size:'100% 100%'};
    else {const tex=currentTexture();entry={name:cleanName(name,'My Texture'),css:tex.css,size:tex.size};}
    if(!safeCss(entry.css)){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Please choose a valid effect');return;}
    if(savePreset){const key=editorKind==='gradient'?GRAD_KEY:TEX_KEY,list=readList(key);list.unshift(entry);const dedup=[];for(const it of list){if(!dedup.some(v=>v.css===it.css&&v.size===it.size))dedup.push(it);}writeList(key,dedup);}
    if(editorKind==='gradient')window.ethvellePicsartSetGradient(entry.css);else window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture(entry.css,entry.size);
    closeEditor();if(savePreset&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetTool==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetTool(editorKind);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(savePreset?'Custom preset saved and applied':'Custom effect applied',{duration:1200});
  }

  // Match the reference: a large color-filled loupe with a centered crosshair, while sampling
  // the actual pixel beneath the user's finger. This works when the native EyeDropper API is absent.
  function containsStage(el,stage){return !!el&&!!stage&&(el===stage||stage.contains(el));}
  function rgbaToHex(v){const m=String(v||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)/i);if(!m)return null;return '#'+m.slice(1,4).map(n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Number(n))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
  function sampleCanvas(el,x,y){try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height)return null;const px=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.width-1,Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width))),py=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.height-1,Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height)));const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(!d||d[3]<8)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(n=>n.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function sampleImage(el,x,y){try{const nw=el.tagName==='VIDEO'?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=el.tagName==='VIDEO'?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);if(!nw||!nh||(el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&el.readyState<2))return null;const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;const scale=Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh),sx=Math.max(0,Math.min(nw-1,Math.floor((x-r.left-(r.width-nw*scale)/2)/scale))),sy=Math.max(0,Math.min(nh-1,Math.floor((y-r.top-(r.height-nh*scale)/2)/scale)));const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});ctx.drawImage(el,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=ctx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;if(d[3]<8)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(n=>n.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}catch(_e){return null;}}
  function readCanvasColor(x,y){const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return null;let stack=[];try{stack=document.elementsFromPoint(x,y).filter(el=>containsStage(el,stage));}catch(_e){}for(const el of stack){if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){const c=sampleCanvas(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}}for(const el of stack){if(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'){const c=sampleImage(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}}for(const el of stack){const cs=getComputedStyle(el);const c=rgbaToHex(cs.backgroundColor);if(c&&Number(cs.opacity||1)>.08)return c;if(el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){const t=rgbaToHex(cs.color);if(t)return t;}}let n=stack[0]||stage;while(n&&stage.contains(n)){const c=rgbaToHex(getComputedStyle(n).backgroundColor);if(c)return c;if(n===stage)break;n=n.parentElement;}return rgbaToHex(getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor);}
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    const key=(typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'&&window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget())||'textColor';
    const rootIds=['ethvelleTextComposer','ethvelleColorPicker','ethvelleTextStylePanel','ethvellePicsartTextDock'];
    const saved=rootIds.map(id=>$(id)).filter(Boolean).map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility}));saved.forEach(x=>x.el.style.visibility='hidden');
    let lens=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!lens){lens=document.createElement('div');lens.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';document.body.appendChild(lens);}lens.innerHTML='<i aria-hidden="true"></i><b aria-hidden="true">+</b><span class="evpa-loupe-hex">#777777</span>';lens.style.display='block';lens.style.backgroundColor='#777777';
    let pointerId=null,lastColor=null,finished=false;
    const hexLabel=lens.querySelector('.evpa-loupe-hex');
    const overStage=(x,y)=>{const el=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);return containsStage(el,stage);};
    const setLens=(x,y)=>{lens.style.left=x+'px';lens.style.top=y+'px';const color=readCanvasColor(x,y);if(color){lastColor=color;lens.style.backgroundColor=color;if(hexLabel)hexLabel.textContent=color;}else if(hexLabel)hexLabel.textContent=' - ';};
    const prevent=e=>{try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();}catch(_e){}};
    const cleanup=()=>{if(finished)return;finished=true;document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',down,true);document.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);document.removeEventListener('pointerup',up,true);document.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);lens.style.display='none';saved.forEach(x=>x.el.style.visibility=x.visibility);if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')try{window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(_e){};};
    const down=e=>{if(pointerId!==null||!overStage(e.clientX,e.clientY))return;pointerId=e.pointerId;prevent(e);setLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const move=e=>{if(pointerId===null||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;prevent(e);setLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const up=e=>{if(pointerId===null||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;prevent(e);const inside=overStage(e.clientX,e.clientY);setLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);const color=inside?(readCanvasColor(e.clientX,e.clientY)||lastColor):null;cleanup();if(color&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});}else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Choose a readable pixel inside the canvas',{duration:1500});const pickerWasOpen=$('ethvelleColorPicker')?.classList.contains('active');if(pickerWasOpen&&color&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')window.setTimeout(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key),40);};
    const cancel=()=>cleanup();const keyCancel=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){cleanup();}};
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown',down,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointerup',up,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Touch and move on the canvas; release to pick the color',{duration:1900});
  };

  // Vertical drags inside the control list scroll the settings sheet. Horizontal gestures on a
  // range keep their native slider behavior, so touch scrolling no longer steals adjustments.
  // v189: the controls list now scrolls natively (touch-action:pan-y). The old handler below
  // set scrollTop by hand on every touchmove and called preventDefault(), which disabled
  // momentum scrolling and made the text panel stutter. Intentionally a no-op.
  function installVerticalControlScroll(){}
  function boot(){installVerticalControlScroll();const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock){const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>installVerticalControlScroll());observer.observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true});}}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-workflow-v12-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const originalSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  const originalRender=window.renderOverlayLayers;
  let samplerOn=false, activePointer=null, lastPoint=null, lastSample=null, hideState=[];
  const rangeGestures=new WeakMap();
  const corsImages=new Map();

  function overlayGroupFor(clip){
    if(!clip)return null;
    return ethvelleOverlayGroupKey(clip);
  }
  function syncSelectedLayerBadges(){
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
      document.querySelectorAll('.layer-row:not(.char-row)').forEach(row=>row.classList.remove('selected'));
      const bgBadge=$('bgLayerBadge'),bgRow=$('backgroundRow');
      if(bgBadge)bgBadge.classList.remove('active');
      const bg=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds.find(b=>String(b.id)===id):null;
      if(bg){if(bgRow)bgRow.classList.add('selected');if(bgBadge)bgBadge.classList.add('active');}
      const ov=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id):null;
      if(ov){const key=overlayGroupFor(ov);const row=document.querySelector(`.overlay-track-row[data-group="${key}"]`);if(row)row.classList.add('selected');}
      // A direct tap on the Background badge also highlights it when no background clip exists yet.
      if(!id&&typeof selectedLayer!=='undefined'&&selectedLayer==='background'){
        if(bgRow)bgRow.classList.add('selected');if(bgBadge)bgBadge.classList.add('active');
      }
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  if(typeof originalSelect==='function'){
    window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const out=originalSelect.apply(this,arguments);syncSelectedLayerBadges();return out;};
  }
  const originalDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof originalDeselect==='function'){
    window.deselectAllClips=function(){const out=originalDeselect.apply(this,arguments);if(typeof selectedLayer!=='undefined')selectedLayer=null;syncSelectedLayerBadges();return out;};
  }
  if(typeof originalRender==='function'){
    window.renderOverlayLayers=function(){const out=originalRender.apply(this,arguments);syncSelectedLayerBadges();return out;};
  }
  // Make sure a text row's selected state survives redraws and direct timeline taps.
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
    if(e.target.closest?.('#timelineInner .overlay-clip,#overlayRows .layer-badge,#track-background .track-clip,#bgLayerBadge'))setTimeout(syncSelectedLayerBadges,0);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target.closest?.('#backgroundRow .layer-badge')){
      const bg=$('backgroundRow'),badge=$('bgLayerBadge');bg?.classList.add('selected');badge?.classList.add('active');
    }
  },true);

  // Text adjustments: require a deliberate drag that begins on the slider thumb.
  // Native range inputs jump on track taps in some Android WebViews, so this scoped handler
  // owns the gesture and updates values only while the thumb is being dragged.
  let activeRangeDrag=null;
  function sliderScope(el){return !!el?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody');}
  function rangeGeometry(input){
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pad=Math.min(12,Math.max(7,r.width*0.08));
    const start=r.left+pad,end=Math.max(start+1,r.right-pad);
    const min=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.min))?parseFloat(input.min):0;
    const max=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.max))?parseFloat(input.max):100;
    const value=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.value))?parseFloat(input.value):min;
    const fraction=max===min?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(value-min)/(max-min)));
    return {start,end,min,max,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1),thumbX:start+fraction*(end-start),pad};
  }
  function assignRangeFromX(input,x,g){
    // Reuse the pointer-down geometry. Calling getBoundingClientRect() on every move
    // forced synchronous layout while the finger was sliding a range control.
    g=g||rangeGeometry(input);
    const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-g.start)/(g.end-g.start)));
    let v=g.min+ratio*(g.max-g.min);
    if(g.step>0)v=g.min+Math.round((v-g.min)/g.step)*g.step;
    v=Math.max(g.min,Math.min(g.max,v));
    const decimals=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
    const next=String(Number(v.toFixed(Math.min(5,decimals))));
    if(input.value!==next){input.value=next;input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const input=e.target?.closest?.('input[type="range"]');
    if(!input||!sliderScope(input))return;
    const g=rangeGeometry(input),nearThumb=Math.abs(e.clientX-g.thumbX)<=Math.max(8,Math.min(11,g.pad-1));
    // Prevent browser-specific track-click jumps even when the press is not on the thumb.
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
    if(!nearThumb)return;
    activeRangeDrag={input,pointerId:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,lastX:e.clientX,startValue:input.value,moved:false,geometry:g,raf:0};
    try{input.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    const drag=activeRangeDrag;if(!drag||drag.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
    drag.lastX=e.clientX;
    if(Math.abs(e.clientX-drag.startX)>2)drag.moved=true;
    if(drag.moved&&!drag.raf){
      drag.raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        drag.raf=0;
        if(activeRangeDrag===drag&&drag.moved)assignRangeFromX(drag.input,drag.lastX,drag.geometry);
      });
    }
  },true);
  function finishRangeDrag(e){
    const drag=activeRangeDrag;if(!drag||drag.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    e.preventDefault?.();e.stopPropagation?.();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();
    if(drag.raf)cancelAnimationFrame(drag.raf);
    drag.raf=0;activeRangeDrag=null;
    if(drag.moved){assignRangeFromX(drag.input,drag.lastX,drag.geometry);drag.input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
    else drag.input.value=drag.startValue;
    try{drag.input.releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',finishRangeDrag,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(activeRangeDrag&&activeRangeDrag.pointerId===e.pointerId){activeRangeDrag.input.value=activeRangeDrag.startValue;activeRangeDrag=null;}},true);
  function parseRGBA(value){
    const m=String(value||'').match(/rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)(?:\s*[,/]\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(!m)return null;let a=m[4]===undefined?1:parseFloat(m[4]);if(String(m[4]||'').includes('%'))a/=100;
    if(a<=0.025)return null;
    return '#'+m.slice(1,4).map(v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(Number(v)))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
  }
  function sampleCanvas(el,x,y){
    try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||!el.width||!el.height)return null;const px=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.width-1,Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width))),py=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.height-1,Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height)));const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(!d||d[3]<10)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function sampleLoadedImage(el,x,y){
    try{
      const isVideo=el.tagName==='VIDEO',nw=isVideo?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=isVideo?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);if(!nw||!nh||(isVideo&&el.readyState<2))return null;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom||!r.width||!r.height)return null;
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el),fit=cs.objectFit||'fill';let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;
      if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'){const scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);const rw=nw*scale,rh=nh*scale,ox=(r.width-rw)/2,oy=(r.height-rh)/2;sx=(x-r.left-ox)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-oy)/scale;}
      sx=Math.max(0,Math.min(nw-1,Math.floor(sx)));sy=Math.max(0,Math.min(nh-1,Math.floor(sy)));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const ctx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});ctx.drawImage(el,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=ctx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;if(d[3]<10)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function sampleCorsClone(el,x,y){
    try{
      const src=el.currentSrc||el.src;if(!src||!/^https?:|^data:|^blob:/i.test(src))return null;
      let entry=corsImages.get(src);
      if(!entry){const image=new Image();image.crossOrigin='anonymous';entry={image,ready:false,failed:false};corsImages.set(src,entry);image.onload=()=>{entry.ready=true;try{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const cx=c.getContext('2d');cx.drawImage(image,0,0,1,1);cx.getImageData(0,0,1,1);}catch(_e){entry.failed=true;}};image.onerror=()=>{entry.failed=true;};image.src=src;return null;}
      if(!entry.ready||entry.failed)return null;
      const image=entry.image,isVideo=el.tagName==='VIDEO',nw=isVideo?(el.videoWidth||0):(el.naturalWidth||0),nh=isVideo?(el.videoHeight||0):(el.naturalHeight||0);if(!nw||!nh)return null;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el),fit=cs.objectFit||'fill';let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;
      if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'){const scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);const rw=nw*scale,rh=nh*scale;sx=(x-r.left-(r.width-rw)/2)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-(r.height-rh)/2)/scale;}
      sx=Math.max(0,Math.min(nw-1,Math.floor(sx)));sy=Math.max(0,Math.min(nh-1,Math.floor(sy)));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const cx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});cx.drawImage(image,sx,sy,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=cx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;if(d[3]<10)return null;return '#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function sampleTextColor(el,x,y){
    try{
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);const fill=String(cs.webkitTextFillColor||'');const bg=String(cs.backgroundImage||'');
      // For gradient-filled text, choose the nearest visible gradient stop instead of an often-transparent `color` value.
      if(bg&&bg.includes('gradient')){
        const stops=Array.from(bg.matchAll(/(#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|rgba?\([^)]*\))/ig)).map(m=>m[1]);
        if(stops.length){const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(.999,(x-r.left)/Math.max(1,r.width)));const idx=Math.min(stops.length-1,Math.floor(ratio*stops.length));const c=stops[idx].startsWith('#')?stops[idx]:parseRGBA(stops[idx]);if(c&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c))return c.toUpperCase();}
      }
      if(fill&&fill!=='transparent'&&fill!=='rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'){const c=parseRGBA(fill)||(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(fill)?fill.toUpperCase():null);if(c)return c;}
      const c=parseRGBA(cs.color)||(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(cs.color)?cs.color.toUpperCase():null);if(c)return c;
    }catch(_e){}
    return null;
  }
  function sampleDomElement(el,x,y){
    if(!el||el.nodeType!==1)return null;
    if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){const c=sampleCanvas(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    if(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'){const c=sampleLoadedImage(el,x,y)||sampleCorsClone(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    if(el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){const c=sampleTextColor(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    // CSS gradient backgrounds such as the stage itself are handled by a stop-color approximation.
    if(String(cs.backgroundImage||'').includes('gradient')){
      const stops=Array.from(String(cs.backgroundImage).matchAll(/(#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|rgba?\([^)]*\))/ig)).map(m=>m[1]);
      if(stops.length){const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(.999,(x-r.left)/Math.max(1,r.width)));const raw=stops[Math.min(stops.length-1,Math.floor(ratio*stops.length))];const c=raw.startsWith('#')?raw.toUpperCase():parseRGBA(raw);if(c&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c))return c;}
    }
    const bg=parseRGBA(cs.backgroundColor);if(bg&&Number(cs.opacity||1)>.025)return bg;
    if(el.tagName==='SVG'||el.tagName==='PATH'){const stroke=parseRGBA(cs.stroke)||parseRGBA(cs.fill);if(stroke)return stroke;}
    return null;
  }
  function inStage(x,y){const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return false;const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();return x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom;}
  function readStageColor(x,y){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!inStage(x,y))return null;
    let stack=[];try{stack=document.elementsFromPoint(x,y);}catch(_e){}
    // Walk in front-to-back paint order, restricted to the stage. Transparent canvas pixels fall through to media/text/background below.
    for(const el of stack){if(!(el===stage||stage.contains(el)))continue;const c=sampleDomElement(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    // Pointer-events:none media can be omitted from elementsFromPoint(). Inspect overlapping
    // stage descendants back-to-front as a fallback so those image/video layers are still sampleable.
    const covers=(el)=>{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom&&r.width>0&&r.height>0;};
    const latent=Array.from(stage.querySelectorAll('canvas,img,video,.ethvelle-overlay-text-content')).reverse();
    for(const el of latent){if(!covers(el))continue;const c=sampleDomElement(el,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    // Specific fallback for the always-present background image and the stage's CSS fill.
    const bg=$('bgImgLayer');if(bg&&covers(bg)){const c=sampleLoadedImage(bg,x,y)||sampleCorsClone(bg,x,y);if(c)return c;}
    const stageColor=parseRGBA(getComputedStyle(stage).backgroundColor);if(stageColor)return stageColor;
    return null;
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    if(samplerOn)return;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    samplerOn=true;activePointer=null;lastPoint=null;lastSample=null;
    const key=(typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'&&window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget())||'textColor';
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker'),composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),stylePanel=$('ethvelleTextStylePanel'),dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    const roots=[picker,composer,stylePanel,dock].filter(Boolean);hideState=roots.map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility,display:el.style.display}));
    roots.forEach(el=>{el.style.visibility='hidden';});
    // Keep the originating panel's active state intact; visibility is restored after sampling.
    let lens=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!lens){lens=document.createElement('div');lens.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';document.body.appendChild(lens);}
    lens.innerHTML='<i aria-hidden="true"></i><b aria-hidden="true"></b>';lens.style.display='block';lens.style.backgroundColor='#777777';lens.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color','#777777');
    const moveLens=(x,y)=>{
      lastPoint={x,y};lens.style.left=x+'px';lens.style.top=y+'px';
      if(inStage(x,y)){const c=readStageColor(x,y);if(c){lastSample=c;lens.style.backgroundColor=c;lens.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color',c);}}
    };
    const prevent=e=>{try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();}catch(_e){}};
    const cleanup=()=>{
      document.removeEventListener('pointerdown',down,true);document.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);document.removeEventListener('pointerup',up,true);document.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);
      lens.style.display='none';samplerOn=false;activePointer=null;hideState.forEach(o=>{o.el.style.visibility=o.visibility;o.el.style.display=o.display;});hideState=[];
      if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')try{window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(_e){}
      if(pickerWasOpen&&picker){picker.classList.add('active');picker.style.visibility='';}
    };
    const pickerWasOpen=!!picker?.classList.contains('active');
    const down=e=>{if(activePointer!==null)return;activePointer=e.pointerId;prevent(e);moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const move=e=>{if(activePointer===null||e.pointerId!==activePointer)return;prevent(e);moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const up=e=>{
      if(activePointer===null||e.pointerId!==activePointer)return;prevent(e);moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);
      const color=inStage(e.clientX,e.clientY)?(readStageColor(e.clientX,e.clientY)||lastSample):null;cleanup();
      if(color){if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);else if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue(key,color);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});}
      else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Move the crosshair onto a readable canvas pixel and release',{duration:1500});
      if(color&&pickerWasOpen&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')setTimeout(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key),40);
    };
    const cancel=()=>cleanup();const keyCancel=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')cleanup();};
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown',down,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointerup',up,{capture:true,passive:false});document.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Drag the crosshair over the canvas; the outer ring previews the color live',{duration:1800});
  };

  // Remove the X affordance and add a proper bottom Close action to the selected-text settings dock.
  function closeTextEditing(){
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(composer?.classList.contains('active')&&typeof window.closeEthvelleTextComposer==='function')window.closeEthvelleTextComposer(false);
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker');picker?.classList.remove('active');
    const style=$('ethvelleTextStylePanel');style?.classList.remove('active','evts-compose-subpanel');
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock){dock.style.display='none';dock.classList.remove('evpa-in-composer');}
    if(typeof window.deselectAllClips==='function')window.deselectAllClips();
    else if(typeof deselectAllClips==='function')deselectAllClips();
    if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();
    syncSelectedLayerBadges();
  }
  function ensureDockFooter(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    const x=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-head .evpa-sheet-close');if(x)x.style.display='none';
    if(!dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-footer')){
      const footer=document.createElement('div');footer.className='evpa-sheet-footer';footer.innerHTML='<button type="button" aria-label="Close text editing">Close editing</button>';
      footer.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();closeTextEditing();});
      dock.appendChild(footer);
    }
  }
  function boot(){
    syncSelectedLayerBadges();ensureDockFooter();
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock){new MutationObserver(ensureDockFooter).observe(dock,{childList:true,subtree:true});}
    // Existing text tools and settings sheets can be created after DOM ready.
    const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{ensureDockFooter();syncSelectedLayerBadges();});mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-workflow-v13-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const corsCache=new Map();
  let sampling=false, pointerId=null, lastColor=null, hiddenRoots=[], sampleKey='textColor';
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const toHex=(r,g,b)=>'#'+[r,g,b].map(v=>clamp(Math.round(v),0,255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();
  function parsePaint(v){
    const s=String(v||'').trim();
    if(!s||s==='transparent')return null;
    const h=s.match(/^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{4}|[0-9a-f]{6}|[0-9a-f]{8})$/i);
    if(h){let q=h[1];if(q.length<=4)q=q.split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');return {r:parseInt(q.slice(0,2),16),g:parseInt(q.slice(2,4),16),b:parseInt(q.slice(4,6),16),a:q.length===8?parseInt(q.slice(6,8),16)/255:1};}
    const m=s.match(/rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)\s*[, ]\s*([\d.]+)(?:\s*[,/]\s*([\d.]+%?))?\s*\)/i);
    if(m){let a=m[4]===undefined?1:parseFloat(m[4]);if(String(m[4]||'').includes('%'))a/=100;return {r:clamp(+m[1],0,255),g:clamp(+m[2],0,255),b:clamp(+m[3],0,255),a:clamp(a,0,1)};}
    return null;
  }
  function visible(el){if(!el||!el.isConnected)return false;const cs=getComputedStyle(el);return cs.display!=='none'&&cs.visibility!=='hidden'&&Number(cs.opacity||1)>.001;}
  function ancestorOpacity(el){let a=1;for(let n=el;n&&n.nodeType===1;n=n.parentElement){const op=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(n).opacity);if(Number.isFinite(op))a*=clamp(op,0,1);if(n.id==='canvasStage')break;}return a;}
  function insideRect(el,x,y){const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.height>0&&x>=r.left&&x<=r.right&&y>=r.top&&y<=r.bottom;}
  function imageCoords(el,x,y,nw,nh){
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();if(!r.width||!r.height||x<r.left||x>r.right||y<r.top||y>r.bottom)return null;
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el),fit=cs.objectFit||'fill';let sx=(x-r.left)/r.width*nw,sy=(y-r.top)/r.height*nh;
    if(fit==='cover'||fit==='contain'||fit==='scale-down'){
      let scale=fit==='cover'?Math.max(r.width/nw,r.height/nh):Math.min(r.width/nw,r.height/nh);
      if(fit==='scale-down')scale=Math.min(1,scale);
      const dw=nw*scale,dh=nh*scale,ox=(r.width-dw)/2,oy=(r.height-dh)/2;
      if(fit!=='cover'&&(x-r.left<ox||x-r.left>ox+dw||y-r.top<oy||y-r.top>oy+dh))return null;
      sx=(x-r.left-ox)/scale;sy=(y-r.top-oy)/scale;
    }
    const pos=String(cs.objectPosition||'50% 50%').split(/\s+/);
    // Current stage images use center object-position. The calculations above deliberately
    // preserve that common case and still handle transparent contain margins accurately.
    return {x:clamp(Math.floor(sx),0,nw-1),y:clamp(Math.floor(sy),0,nh-1)};
  }
  function readSourcePixel(source,el,x,y){
    const isVideo=el.tagName==='VIDEO',nw=isVideo?(source.videoWidth||source.naturalWidth||0):(source.naturalWidth||source.width||0),nh=isVideo?(source.videoHeight||source.naturalHeight||0):(source.naturalHeight||source.height||0);
    if(!nw||!nh||(isVideo&&source.readyState<2))return {blocked:true,pending:true};
    const p=imageCoords(el,x,y,nw,nh);if(!p)return null;
    try{const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=1;const cx=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});cx.drawImage(source,p.x,p.y,1,1,0,0,1,1);const d=cx.getImageData(0,0,1,1).data;return {r:d[0],g:d[1],b:d[2],a:d[3]/255};}
    catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function corsPixel(el,x,y){
    if(el.tagName==='VIDEO')return {blocked:true};
    const src=el.currentSrc||el.src;if(!src)return {blocked:true};
    let entry=corsCache.get(src);
    if(!entry){
      const image=new Image();image.crossOrigin='anonymous';
      entry={image,state:'loading',promise:null};
      entry.promise=new Promise(resolve=>{image.onload=()=>{entry.state='ready';resolve(true);};image.onerror=()=>{entry.state='failed';resolve(false);};});
      corsCache.set(src,entry);image.src=src;
      return {blocked:true,promise:entry.promise};
    }
    if(entry.state==='loading')return {blocked:true,promise:entry.promise};
    if(entry.state!=='ready')return {blocked:true};
    return readSourcePixel(entry.image,el,x,y)||{blocked:true};
  }
  function imagePixel(el,x,y){
    if(!visible(el)||!insideRect(el,x,y))return null;
    const p=readSourcePixel(el,el,x,y);
    if(p&&p.blocked)return p;
    if(p)return p;
    // If sampling the already-rendered asset taints a temporary canvas, try a CORS-enabled
    // clone. A remote host that forbids CORS is a browser security boundary and cannot expose
    // exact pixels to HTML/JavaScript; report that instead of silently picking a layer below.
    return corsPixel(el,x,y);
  }
  function gradientPaint(el,x,y,bg){
    const str=String(bg||'');if(!str.includes('gradient'))return null;
    const stops=Array.from(str.matchAll(/(#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|rgba?\([^)]*\))/ig)).map(m=>parsePaint(m[1])).filter(Boolean);
    if(!stops.length)return null;
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),ratio=clamp((x-r.left)/Math.max(1,r.width),0,.999),idx=Math.min(stops.length-1,Math.floor(ratio*stops.length));return stops[idx];
  }
  function styledPixel(el,x,y,onlyBg=false){
    if(!visible(el)||!insideRect(el,x,y))return null;
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    if(!onlyBg&&el.classList?.contains('ethvelle-overlay-text-content')){
      let paint=null;const bg=String(cs.backgroundImage||'');
      if(bg.includes('gradient'))paint=gradientPaint(el,x,y,bg);
      if(!paint)paint=parsePaint(cs.webkitTextFillColor)||parsePaint(cs.color);
      if(paint){paint={...paint,a:paint.a*ancestorOpacity(el)};return paint;}
    }
    if(!onlyBg&&el.classList?.contains('overlay-text-render')){
      const text=el.querySelector('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');
      if(text&&insideRect(text,x,y)){const p=styledPixel(text,x,y,false);if(p)return p;}
    }
    const grad=gradientPaint(el,x,y,cs.backgroundImage);if(grad){grad.a*=ancestorOpacity(el);return grad;}
    let p=parsePaint(cs.backgroundColor);
    if(p&&p.a>.001){p={...p,a:p.a*ancestorOpacity(el)};return p;}
    if(!onlyBg&&(el.tagName==='SVG'||el.tagName==='PATH'||el.tagName==='RECT'||el.tagName==='CIRCLE')){
      p=parsePaint(cs.fill)||parsePaint(cs.stroke);if(p&&p.a>.001){p={...p,a:p.a*ancestorOpacity(el)};return p;}
    }
    return null;
  }
  function sampleLayer(el,x,y){
    if(!visible(el)||!insideRect(el,x,y))return null;
    if(el.tagName==='CANVAS'){
      try{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();const px=clamp(Math.floor((x-r.left)*el.width/r.width),0,el.width-1),py=clamp(Math.floor((y-r.top)*el.height/r.height),0,el.height-1);const d=el.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true})?.getImageData(px,py,1,1).data;if(d)return {r:d[0],g:d[1],b:d[2],a:(d[3]/255)*ancestorOpacity(el)};}
      catch(_e){return null;}return null;
    }
    if(el.tagName==='IMG'||el.tagName==='VIDEO'){
      const p=imagePixel(el,x,y);if(!p)return null;if(p.blocked)return p;p.a*=ancestorOpacity(el);return p;
    }
    return styledPixel(el,x,y,false);
  }
  function blend(over,under){
    const a=over.a+under.a*(1-over.a);if(a<=.001)return {r:0,g:0,b:0,a:0};
    return {r:(over.r*over.a+under.r*under.a*(1-over.a))/a,g:(over.g*over.a+under.g*under.a*(1-over.a))/a,b:(over.b*over.a+under.b*under.a*(1-over.a))/a,a};
  }
  function asHex(p){return p&&p.a>.015?toHex(p.r,p.g,p.b):null;}
  function stageCandidates(stage){
    const arr=[],canvas=$('stickCanvas'),media=$('overlayMediaLayer'),bg=$('bgImgLayer');
    // Actual stage paint order, top to bottom: the character canvas, newest overlay media/text,
    // older overlay media/text, background image, then the stage's own color.
    if(canvas)arr.push(canvas);
    if(media){const kids=Array.from(media.children);for(let i=kids.length-1;i>=0;i--)arr.push(kids[i]);}
    if(bg)arr.push(bg);
    arr.push(stage);return arr;
  }
  function readVisibleColor(x,y){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage||!insideRect(stage,x,y))return {color:null};
    const translucent=[];
    for(const el of stageCandidates(stage)){
      if(el!==stage&&!stage.contains(el))continue;
      if(el===stage){
        const base=styledPixel(stage,x,y,true)||{r:0,g:0,b:0,a:1};
        let result=base;for(let i=translucent.length-1;i>=0;i--)result=blend(translucent[i],result);
        return {color:asHex(result)};
      }
      const p=sampleLayer(el,x,y);if(!p)continue;
      if(p.blocked)return {color:null,blocked:true,promise:p.promise};
      if(p.a<=.01)continue;
      if(p.a>=.995){let result=p;for(let i=translucent.length-1;i>=0;i--)result=blend(translucent[i],result);return {color:asHex(result)};}
      translucent.push(p);
    }
    return {color:null};
  }
  function insideStage(x,y){const s=$('canvasStage');return !!s&&insideRect(s,x,y);}
  window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor=function(){
    if(sampling)return;
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas is not available');return;}
    sampling=true;pointerId=null;lastColor=null;
    sampleKey=(typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget==='function'&&window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget())||'textColor';
    const ids=['ethvelleColorPicker','ethvelleTextComposer','ethvelleTextStylePanel','ethvellePicsartTextDock'];
    hiddenRoots=ids.map(id=>$(id)).filter(Boolean).map(el=>({el,visibility:el.style.visibility,display:el.style.display}));
    hiddenRoots.forEach(o=>{o.el.style.visibility='hidden';});
    const picker=$('ethvelleColorPicker'),pickerWasOpen=!!picker?.classList.contains('active');
    let lens=$('evpaSamplerLoupe');if(!lens){lens=document.createElement('div');lens.id='evpaSamplerLoupe';document.body.appendChild(lens);}
    // No literal plus glyph and no color-number badge; the center is fully transparent.
    lens.innerHTML='<b aria-hidden="true"></b>';lens.style.display='block';lens.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color','#777777');
    const prevent=e=>{try{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation?.();}catch(_e){}};
    const placeLens=(x,y)=>{
      const size=lens.getBoundingClientRect().width||144,half=size/2;
      // Keep the hollow crosshair centered on the exact canvas point being sampled while
      // clamping the circle inside the viewport so it never gets clipped off-screen.
      const left=clamp(x,half+2,Math.max(half+2,window.innerWidth-half-2));
      const top=clamp(y,half+2,Math.max(half+2,window.innerHeight-half-2));
      lens.style.left=left+'px';lens.style.top=top+'px';
    };
    const moveLens=(x,y)=>{
      placeLens(x,y);const result=readVisibleColor(x,y);
      if(result.color){lastColor=result.color;lens.style.setProperty('--evpa-sample-color',result.color);}
      return result;
    };
    let ended=false;
    const cleanup=()=>{
      if(ended)return;ended=true;
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown',down,true);window.removeEventListener('pointermove',move,true);window.removeEventListener('pointerup',up,true);window.removeEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);
      lens.style.display='none';sampling=false;pointerId=null;
      hiddenRoots.forEach(o=>{o.el.style.visibility=o.visibility;o.el.style.display=o.display;});hiddenRoots=[];
      if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')try{window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(_e){}
      if(pickerWasOpen&&picker){picker.classList.add('active');picker.style.visibility='';}
    };
    const down=e=>{if(pointerId!==null||!insideStage(e.clientX,e.clientY))return;pointerId=e.pointerId;prevent(e);try{e.target?.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)}catch(_e){}moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const move=e=>{if(pointerId===null||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;prevent(e);moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);};
    const up=async e=>{
      if(pointerId===null||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;prevent(e);
      let result=insideStage(e.clientX,e.clientY)?moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY):{color:null};
      if(result.blocked){if(result.promise)await Promise.race([result.promise,new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,900))]);else if(result.pending)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,350));if(insideStage(e.clientX,e.clientY))result=moveLens(e.clientX,e.clientY);}
      const color=insideStage(e.clientX,e.clientY)?(result.blocked?null:(result.color||lastColor)):null;
      const blocked=!!result.blocked;cleanup();
      if(color){if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(sampleKey,color);else if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue(sampleKey,color);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{duration:1100});}
      else if(blocked){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(result.pending?'This media is still loading. Try sampling again once it is ready.':'This media blocks pixel access in the browser (CORS protection)',{duration:2100});}
      else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Move the crosshair over a visible canvas color and release',{duration:1500});
      if(color&&pickerWasOpen&&typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')setTimeout(()=>window.openEthvelleColorPicker(sampleKey),40);
    };
    const cancel=()=>cleanup(),keyCancel=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')cleanup();};
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown',down,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointermove',move,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointerup',up,{capture:true,passive:false});window.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancel,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',keyCancel,true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Drag the crosshair across the visible canvas; the outer ring previews the sampled color',{duration:1700});
  };

  // After the text-layer has actually re-rendered, refresh its snug frame. This complements
  // the offsetWidth/offsetHeight fix in selBoxTarget so scaled text isn't double-scaled.
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function'&&!priorSelect.__ethvelleV13){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}});return out;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV13=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  function reorderCloseFooter(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    let footer=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-footer');const tabs=dock.querySelector('.evpa-tabs');
    if(footer&&tabs&&footer.nextElementSibling!==tabs)dock.insertBefore(footer,tabs);
    const close=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-head .evpa-sheet-close');if(close)close.remove();
  }
  function boot(){
    reorderCloseFooter();const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dock)new MutationObserver(reorderCloseFooter).observe(dock,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle stability patch v14: animation badge state, text canvas dragging, bottom close tab, sampler ring -->
<style id="ethvelle-text-stability-v14-css">
